HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000202/B001","root_000299/B006","root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0a53c97e965d3f610e3c","text":"1. **Earth as container made to disgorge hidden liabilities.** Lexical evidence: `خ ر ج B002` causing something to emerge from hiddenness; `ء ر ض B001` lower ground; `ث ق ل B001` heaviness; activated by `ح د ث B006` disclosure in 99:4, `خ ب ر B001` inner/tested knowledge in 99:4, and `ر ء ي B012` showing/making seen in 99:6-8. Structural cue: the earth both undergoes shaking in 99:1 and produces contents in 99:2 before becoming a reporter in 99:4. Assigned role: “burdens” are the hidden weight that must move from containment to evidence. Abductive move: I infer that the hidden contents are not merely physical objects but liabilities made evidentiary because the later sequence explicitly makes deeds seen. Reading change: the ayah reads as extraction of weight from concealment into a forensic field.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000202/B004","root_000400/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_11691b7c047c5bafb217","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:2:1\",\"root_000400\",[\"B003\"]],[\"99:2:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B002\"]],[\"99:2:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yerin çıkardıkları, insan emeğinin dünyaya bıraktığının ölçülü ve vadesi gelmiş karşılığı gibi de okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B011","root_000400/B004","root_000787/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_143e7e9b7c47e430f257","text":"4. **Abscess/wound eruption reading.** Lexical evidence: `خ ر ج B004` erupting abscess; `ء ر ض B011` sore festering with pus; activated by `ش ر ر B004` shredding/shaking loose in 99:8 and `ز ل ز ل B001` disturbance in 99:1. Structural cue: shaking then output resembles a corrupted body breaking open. Assigned role: earth functions as a body whose hidden corruption can no longer remain under the surface. Abductive move: I supply the pathological arrow from pressure/disturbance to eruption; this is supported by the packet’s body/earth lexical bridge. Reading change: `أثقالها` can be felt as infected moral-material deposits, not only neutral cargo.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B004","root_000400/B003","root_001046/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2db8f508b585add04e52","text":"3. **Due output/yield as settled account.** Lexical evidence: `خ ر ج B003` due output of wealth/rent/tax/yield; `ث ق ل B004` standard weight and weighing; activated by `ع م ل B004/B005` pay/transaction in 99:6-8 and `ص د ر B005` settling liability on money in 99:6. Structural cue: later “works” are shown to people in measured units as small as a `مثقال ذرة`. Assigned role: the earth’s outputs are like dues or yields paid out at a known measure. Abductive move: I infer an accounting mechanism: what was deposited in the earth/world is returned as quantifiable weight. Reading change: the ayah reads not only as unloading but as the world paying out its stored account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001","root_000202/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30844714aaba043ff7bc","text":"2. **The earth's plural loads resolve into exact, minute weights.** Fixed `ث ق ل/B001` supplies heaviness, and `B004` supplies a standard weight and the act of determining it. At the far edge of this focus window, 99:7 repeats the root in `مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ` after 99:6 has named people's deeds (`ع م ل/B001`). The structural movement is from the earth's plural macro-loads to one tiny measured unit. The weight branch assigns `أَثْقَالَهَا` an accounting role. The identification of those loads with assessable human outputs is inferred, but the root recurrence and deed sequence are supplied. The earth's burdens thus become an inventory capable of exact moral measurement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000387/B003","root_000400/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_64abaf564d769b4f4193","text":"4. **The output can be read exploratorily as a due yield.** Fixed `خ ر ج/B003` supplies wealth, yield, levy, or rent paid out in a known amount, while fixed `ء ر ض/B002` supplies fertile ground. The earth's `أَخْبَارَهَا` in 99:4 carries the dormant `خ ب ر/B003` image of cultivating land for a known share of its produce, and 99:6-7 connect the sequence to intentional acts (`ع م ل/B001`) and measurement (`ث ق ل/B004`). The assigned role is agricultural-accounting: the earth yields what human work has put into its domain. The claim that deeds function like cultivated deposits is inferred and makes this reading exploratory. It changes `أَثْقَالَهَا` from undifferentiated burdens into a due, measured return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001","root_000400/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6579c541aaa4f92daa74","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:2:1\",\"root_000400\",[\"B002\"]],[\"99:2:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gizliden çıkarılan ağırlıklar, daha sonra anlatılıp gösterilecek kanıt malzemesine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001","root_000202/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7c207dc3c5e6fde5f670","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:2:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yerin çoğul yükleri, sonraki zerre ölçüsü sayesinde tek tek tartılabilen insan eylemlerinden oluşan bir hesap envanteri olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B011","root_000400/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b0283d9acbb77b9d5e91","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:2:1\",\"root_000400\",[\"B004\"]],[\"99:2:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yeryüzü, altında biriken bozulmayı sarsıntıyla yarılıp dışarı atan hastalıklı bir beden gibi de okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006","root_000400/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b43d2bf70e3b72928539","text":"1. **Extraction begins an evidentiary pipeline.** Fixed `خ ر ج/B002` supplies causing something concealed to come out. Later, 99:4 supplies disclosure (`ح د ث/B006`) and tested inner knowledge reported outward (`خ ب ر/B001`), while 99:6 supplies causative showing (`ر ء ي/B012`). The fixed extraction's role is the material first stage of concealment becoming evidence: emergence, report, then display. The packet supplies those ordered operations; the inference is that they process one underlying record rather than unrelated objects. `أَخْرَجَتِ` therefore reads as disclosure in material form, not only spatial expulsion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B004","root_001633/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bad286549d8ea90cc4fb","text":"99:7-8 made `أثقالها` more exact than “heavy things”: the later `مثقال ذرة` activates `ث ق ل B004`, so earth’s macro-burdens are continuous with tiny measurable moral weights. 99:5 also sharpened the birth/account readings: the earth does not merely expel by natural pressure; it responds to an authorized hidden communication (`و ح ي B001/B004`).","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000202/B007","root_000400/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c28cff613cd76d4a1634","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"99:2:1\",\"root_000400\",[\"B001\"]],[\"99:2:2\",\"root_000025\",[\"B002\"]],[\"99:2:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Üretken yeryüzü, ağırlaşan içeriğini sarsıntılı bir çıkışla doğuran taşıyıcı bir beden görünümü kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c3dc4f00c111a2949e90","text":"99:4 and 99:6 made the expelled contents newly legible as an evidence stream: disclosure and report lead to possessed deeds being shown. The strongest retrospective activation came at 99:7, where the same `ث ق ل` root reappears as `مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ`; this corrected an initial macro-material reading of `أَثْقَالَهَا` by revealing a load that can be resolved into exact individual measures. The cultivation branch in 99:4 also made the exploratory due-yield reading visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B007","root_000400/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_eb80de60fdcab03489ba","text":"2. **Pregnancy/birth reading of earth’s burdens.** Lexical evidence: `ث ق ل B007` pregnancy becoming heavy; `خ ر ج B001/B002` passing out/causing emergence; activated by the feminine earth pronouns and the basmalah’s `ر ح م B003/B004` womb and womb-pain branches. Structural cue: 99:1 shaking, 99:2 خروج, and repeated `ها` produce a sequence like labor: convulsion, bearing weight, emergence. Assigned role: earth is a gravid bearer whose `أثقالها` are what has reached the point of being delivered. Abductive move: I supply the birth-arrow from the branch inventory’s pregnancy/womb images; the packet does not say “birth,” but it couples feminine earth, heaviness, exit, and later disclosed contents. Reading change: the ayah becomes a delivery scene, making the violent upheaval generative as well as destructive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fca550fb27568778dd21","text":"3. **The earth behaves as a gestational vessel that delivers what it carried.** Fixed `ء ر ض/B002` supplies fertile, productive ground, fixed `ث ق ل/B007` supplies pregnancy becoming heavy, and fixed `خ ر ج/B001` supplies emergence from within. The opening `ر ح م/B003` provides the womb image, and 99:7's `ذ ر ر/B004` adds gentle emergence of herbage from earth. Together these material images assign the earth the role of a containing and productive body. The earth-womb equivalence and the treatment of convulsive expulsion as delivery are abductive moves. The ayah consequently permits \"loads\" to be read as carried contents reaching emergence, not merely alien debris being discarded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
